### [1] [Statistics as a local phase: crystalline order and quench dynamics of emergent dimers in Ising gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26264)
*Umberto Borla,Riccardo Cioli,Jad C. Halimeh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在2+1维Z2格点规范理论的强耦合极限下，研究费米子或玻色子电荷被禁闭成紧束缚配对（二聚体）后，其微观统计如何存留。研究发现底层物质的统计性质完全编码在一个局域跳跃相位φ中（玻色子为0，费米子为π），相互作用与统计无关。通过将φ作为连续参数插值，利用张量网络方法绘制了基态相图，发现φ本身驱动二聚体超流与密度波之间的相变，并识别出一种新的有能隙相，其中二聚体对结晶为共振plaquette的有序图案。文章还提出了一个淬火协议，使相同的二聚体构型根据成分统计以不同方式演化，为探测二聚体内部结构和有序相提供动力学手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解微观粒子的玻色/费米统计在强关联系统中被禁闭成复合准粒子后如何存活和表现，是一个基础物理问题。作者希望在格点规范理论的框架下，系统地探究微观统计信息（通过跳跃相位编码）如何影响宏观相图，并寻找能够动力学探测这种内部统计信息的方法。

Method: 在2+1维Z2格点规范理论的强耦合极限下，将禁闭的电荷对映射为有效的二聚体模型。将微观统计编码为单个连续参数φ（硬核玻色子为0，费米子为π），使模型可以在二者之间插值。采用张量网络方法（如无限张量积态等）数值求解基态相图，并设计淬火协议作为动力学探针。

Result: 发现统计信息完全编码在跳跃相位φ中而相互作用与统计无关；φ本身驱动二聚体超流态与二聚体电荷密度波之间的相变。磁耦合使相邻二聚体绑定成共振对，与二聚体间排斥相互作用竞争，从而识别出一个新的有能隙相——二聚体对结晶为有序共振plaquette图案。淬火协议显示相同构型的不同二聚体会因其内在统计产生不同的实时演化，能检测有序相。

Conclusion: 微观玻色/费米统计通过单一跳跃相位在有效二聚体模型中以拓扑/几何方式存活，并直接决定相图和动力学行为。所提出的淬火协议可在原生实现玻色自由度的模拟器（如超冷原子、量子模拟器）中检验，为探测复合粒子的内部统计结构提供了可行途径。

Abstract: How does the Bose or Fermi statistics of microscopic particles survive when confinement binds them into emergent bosonic composites? We address this question in the strong-coupling limit of a $2+1$D $\mathbb{Z}_2$ lattice gauge theory, where charges are confined into tightly bound pairs that can be described by an effective dimer model. We find that the statistics of the underlying matter is encoded entirely in a single local hopping phase $\varphi$ -$0$ for bosons, $π$ for fermions- while interactions remain statistics-independent. Treating $\varphi$ as a continuous parameter that interpolates between the two, we map the ground-state phase diagram with the help of tensor-network methods. The angle $\varphi$ itself drives a transition between a dimer-superfluid and dimer charge density wave state, while the magnetic coupling binds neighboring dimers into resonating pairs, in competition with the inter-dimer repulsion. We identify a novel gapped phase in which dimer pairs crystallize into an ordered pattern of resonating plaquettes. Finally, we propose a quench protocol under which identical dimer configurations evolve in markedly different ways depending on the statistics of their constituents. This provides a dynamical probe of the internal structure of dimers, and detects ordered phases through real time signatures, within reach of simulators that natively realize bosonic degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [2] [Time and Momentum Resolved Tunneling Spectroscopy of Floquet dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26270)
*Lucas Q. Silveira,Adrian E. Feiguin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时间和动量分辨的隧穿光谱协议，用于探测Floquet驱动量子系统的瞬时能谱，超越高频极限，捕获涌现效应和非绝热现象，无需显式重构完整的时间依赖格林函数。该方法在无相互作用费米子系统上基准测试，并通过时间依赖密度矩阵重正化群扩展到强关联系统，同时微观描述了共振驱动下带隙内态的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动量子系统是调控有效哈密顿量以工程化新物质态的有力途径，但现有处理通常简化在高频极限下，未考虑驱动启动过程中的瞬态物理。需要一种方法能够在超越高频极限的情况下探测Floquet系统的瞬时能谱，并捕捉非绝热现象。

Method: 引入时间和动量分辨的隧穿光谱协议，直接探测Floquet驱动系统的瞬时能谱；在无相互作用的费米子系统上基准验证，并使用时间依赖密度矩阵重正化群（tDMRG）技术推广到强关联系统。

Result: 成功基准测试了该方法于驱动非相互作用费米子模型，并推广至强关联系统；对共振驱动下带隙内态的出现提供了微观描述，初步探索了有限温度类比。

Conclusion: 该协议为探测Floquet驱动系统瞬态能谱提供了一种高效方法，无需显式重建完整时间依赖格林函数，能够同时捕捉涌现效应与非绝热现象，并适用于强关联系统及有限温度场景。

Abstract: Periodically driven quantum systems provide a powerful route to engineer novel states of matter by controlling their effective Hamiltonians through external fields. However, most treatments are usually simplified by considering the high-frequency limit, and do not account for the processes taking place in the transient regime during the onset of the drive. In this work, we introduce a time and momentum resolved tunneling spectroscopy protocol to probe the instantaneous energy spectrum of Floquet-driven systems beyond the high-frequency regime, capturing both emergent effects and non-adiabatic phenomena without requiring explicit reconstruction of the full time-dependent Green's function. We benchmark the method on driven non-interacting fermionic models, and then generalize the approach to strongly correlated systems with the aid of time-dependent density matrix renormalization group techniques. We also provide a microscopic description to the emergence of in-gap states under resonant driving, and briefly explore its finite temperature analog.

</details>


### [3] [Dynamical signatures of deconfined spinons in dimerized sawtooth chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26347)
*Nishan Ranabhat,Brandon B. Le,Seung-Hun Lee,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过U(1)对称的DMRG和TDVP方法研究锯齿链上Heisenberg模型在精确可解VBS点及其两侧的基态性质和动力学响应，揭示其激发谱为去禁闭的双自旋子连续谱（由kink与antikink畴壁构成），并给出闭合形式的自旋子色散关系固定连续谱边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解锯齿链Heisenberg模型在二聚化相中的分数化激发机制，特别是在精确VBS点两侧（连续下相边界和一级上相边界）的谱响应行为，并为最近发现的Ti3+ kagome氟化物中锯齿链结构单元提供微观理论依据。

Method: 采用U(1)对称的密度矩阵重正化群(DMRG)计算平衡态诊断量，利用含时变分原理(TDVP)计算零温动力学结构因子S^zz(q,ω)；通过闭合形式的自旋子色散固定连续谱边界，并在约束希尔伯特空间中进行了有限分离双kink的显式计算以复现强度分布。

Result: 在所有参数区间内（接近连续下相边界、精确VBS点和接近一级上相边界），DSF均呈现宽连续谱，其下缘由亮带主导，单triplon能量处的强度被抑制。谱被识别为两个简并单重态覆盖之间的kink与antikink畴壁构成的去禁闭双自旋子连续谱，闭合形式的色散关系三个区间均能追踪主导带，双kink计算准确复现了实验强度分布。

Conclusion: 该工作为锯齿链二聚化相中的分数化激发提供了微观图像，其发现的双自旋子连续谱结构和自旋子色散关系对理解Ti3+ kagome氟化物中强各向异性交换相互作用产生的锯齿链结构块的激发行为具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the ground-state properties and dynamical response of the Heisenberg model on the sawtooth chain in and away from the exactly solvable valence-bond-solid (VBS) point. We employ U(1)-symmetric density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and time-dependent variational principle (TDVP) methods to compute equilibrium diagnostics and the zero-temperature dynamical structure factor (DSF) $S^{zz}(q,ω)$ across the dimerized phase, a valence-bond-ordered state in which the two symmetry-equivalent apex--base bonds of each triangle develop unequal spin correlations, probing the approach to the continuous lower phase boundary, the exact VBS point, and the approach to the first-order upper boundary. In every regime, the DSF is a broad continuum dominated by a bright band at its lower edge, with the intensity at the one-triplon energy $ω\simeq J_{AB}$ suppressed. We identify the spectrum as a deconfined two-spinon continuum of kink and antikink domain walls between the two degenerate singlet coverings. Closed-form spinon dispersions fix the continuum edges and track the dominant band across the zone in all three regimes, while an explicit finite-separation two-kink calculation in a constrained Hilbert space reproduces the measured intensity distribution. Our results provide a microscopic picture of fractionalized excitations in the dimerized sawtooth chain and are relevant to the recently discovered Ti$^{3+}$ kagome fluorides, where strongly anisotropic exchange interactions can generate sawtooth-chain building blocks.

</details>


### [4] [Competition between Geometrical Frustration and the Kondo Effect in CePdAl Revealed by High-Resolution Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26534)
*Yusei Shimizu,Shota Nakamura,Yoichi Ikeda,Yohei Kono,Shunichiro Kittaka,Toshiro Sakakibara,Yosikazu Isikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过高灵敏度磁强计在80 mK下对重费米子化合物CePdAl进行磁性测量，观察到无磁化平台的一级亚磁转变，表明几何阻挫在磁场下通过Kondo效应抑制和有序矩自旋翻转被解除；在4.3 T处发现非线性磁化率异常，提示极化顺磁态中存在非偶极关联；高场区未发现非费米液体行为的证据。这些发现为理解CePdAl中场诱导自旋液体态提供了基本热力学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CePdAl具有准kagome结构，几何阻挫与Kondo效应相互竞争，其磁场诱导的自旋液体态背后的微观机制尚不明确，需要通过精确的磁性测量揭示其热力学特征。

Method: 使用高灵敏度磁强计在80 mK的极低温下对CePdAl进行磁化测量，包括磁化曲线和非线性磁化率的测量，以探测亚磁转变和高场区域的物性行为。

Result: 观察到清晰的一级亚磁转变且无磁化平台；在4.3 T处发现非线性磁化率异常；高场区未发现非费米液体行为证据。

Conclusion: CePdAl在磁场下通过抑制Kondo效应和有序矩的自旋翻转解除几何阻挫，场致自旋液体态具有明确的热力学约束，排除了非费米液体行为的可能性。

Abstract: CePdAl is a heavy-fermion compound with a quasi-kagome structure, where geometrical frustration competes with the Kondo effect. Using a high-sensitivity magnetometer, we observe clear first-order metamagnetic transitions without magnetization plateaus in CePdAl at 80 mK, indicating a lifting of frustration through the suppression of the Kondo effect and spin flips of ordered moments under fields. An anomaly in the nonlinear magnetic susceptibility at 4.3 T suggests nondipolar correlations in the polarized paramagnetic state. Furthermore, we found no evidence of non-Fermi-liquid behavior in the high-field region, where the antiferromagnetic order is completely suppressed. These findings establish the essential thermodynamic constraints for understanding the field-induced spin-liquid state in CePdAl.

</details>


### [5] [Field-amplified readouts of weak altermagnetic exchange in MnF$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26540)
*Guowen Jiang,Feilong Wang,Yunhua Wang,Fawei Zheng,Bin Xi,Hong-Gang Luo,Jize Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对MnF₂这一课本式双亚晶格反铁磁体（也是典型的交变磁体）中难以观测的亚晶格奇交换作用，通过超定第一性原理总能映射确定其符号与量级（第七近邻不平衡δJ₇≈+8 μeV），并发现沿c轴的纵向磁场B∥c可作为该隐藏能标的线性放大器，提供两个带符号的场线性读出：补偿场B*(Q)和固定场下配对能隙劈裂对比。两种读均在0.12 meV能量分辨率下仍然有效，可将μeV量级的交变磁交换纳入现有实验可测范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MnF₂作为经典反铁磁体重新成为交变磁体的原型，但其定义性特征——亚晶格奇交换——由于其只能与偶极-偶极相互作用正交地进入磁振子劈裂，且量级被压制、符号被抹除，因而其存在与量级仍处在激烈争论中。亟需一种方法从理论和实验上明确解析这一隐藏的量级与符号。

Method: 1）采用超定（overdetermined）第一性原理总能映射，精确解析出该交换量级为第七近邻不平衡δJ₇≈+8 μeV；2）显式加入偶极-偶极相互作用的哈密顿量，复现低能隙与可见的有限动量劈裂；3）引入纵向磁场B∥c作为线性放大器，推导两种带符号的场线性读出：一是补偿场B*(Q)（劈裂最小位置），二是固定场下旋转相关伙伴动量处的劈裂对比。

Result: 确定δJ₇≈+8 μeV（带正号）；补偿场|B*|≈0.34 T，其从零场的偏移即证明不平衡存在、其方向给出符号、其大小给出量级；在1 T固定场下配对劈裂对比约为0.14 meV，是零场超出的六倍，可由两个谱直接校验符号。两种读出在0.12 meV能量分辨率下均存活。

Conclusion: 该构造可推广到任意易轴共线交变磁体，使得μeV量级的交变磁交换进入当前实验仪器可及范围，为解决MnF₂交变磁身份之争提供了明确的理论与实验路径。

Abstract: MnF$_2$, the textbook two-sublattice antiferromagnet, has reemerged as a prototypical altermagnet, yet the sublattice-odd exchange that defines this identity remains under active debate: it enters the magnon splitting only in quadrature with the dipole--dipole interaction, its magnitude suppressed and its sign erased. An overdetermined first-principles total-energy mapping resolves this scale as a seventh-neighbor imbalance $δ{J_7}\simeq+8~μ$eV. The resulting Hamiltonian, with the dipole--dipole interaction included explicitly, reproduces the low-energy gap and the visible finite-momentum splitting. A longitudinal field $B\parallel c$ then acts as a linear amplifier of the hidden scale, opening two signed, field-linear readouts. The first is the compensation field $B^\ast(\mathbf Q)$, the position of minimum splitting, which is equal and opposite at the rotation-related partner momenta: a shift from zero field is itself evidence of a finite imbalance, its side gives the sign, and its magnitude, $|B^\ast|\simeq0.34$~T here, gives the scale. The second is the fixed-field contrast of the partner splittings, $\simeq0.14$~meV at $1$~T, six times the zero-field excess: a sign check from just two spectra. Both readouts survive a $0.12$~meV energy resolution, and the construction carries over to any easy-axis collinear altermagnet, bringing $μ$eV altermagnetic exchange within present instrumental reach.

</details>


### [6] [Thermally quenched metastability in metal-insulator transitions via elemental substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26609)
*Hiroshi Oike,Yasunori Takahashi,Keisuke Shibuya,Masaki Nakano,Tatsuki Hanada,Motoaki Hirayama,Hiroko Tokoro,Fumitaka Kagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次证明在金属-绝缘体转变（MIT）中可通过快速热淬火（>10^9 K/s）实现亚稳态的动力学保持。通过钨掺杂VO2，快速冷却动力学上避开了MIT，稳定了长寿命的亚稳态金属相，表明元素替代可将热淬火亚稳态控制扩展到电子相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热淬火已在结构相变（如结构玻璃、淬火合金）中广泛实现亚稳态，但金属-绝缘体转变由集体电子重排而非原子扩散驱动，其能否实现热淬火亚稳态尚属悬而未决的问题，因此需要探索在电子相变中实现类似控制的可行性。

Method: 采用超过10^9 K/s的超快速冷却手段对钨掺杂VO2进行热淬火，通过动力学方式规避金属-绝缘体转变路径，并利用温度依赖的弛豫测量表征亚稳态金属相的稳定性及其动力学特征。

Result: 快速冷却成功稳定了长寿命的亚稳态金属相；温度依赖的弛豫行为显示该过程由成核主导的动力学控制，且钨替代引入了显著的热激活势垒，从而阻碍了向热力学基态的平衡化进程。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了元素替代作为在金属-绝缘体转变中实现热淬火亚稳态的新途径，将亚稳态相控制从结构相扩展到电子相，为电子相调控提供了全新的动力学手段。

Abstract: Thermal quenching inhibits equilibration toward the thermodynamic ground state during phase transitions, revealing metastable phases such as structural glasses and quenched alloys. Whether such thermally quenched metastability can be realized in metal-insulator transitions has remained an open question because these transformations are governed by collective electronic reorganization rather than atomic diffusion. We demonstrate that rapid cooling exceeding 10$^9$ K s$^{-1}$ kinetically avoids the metal-insulator transition, stabilizing a long-lived metastable metallic phase in tungsten-substituted VO$_2$. Temperature-dependent relaxation reveals nucleation-dominated kinetics with a thermal activation barrier introduced by tungsten substitution. Our results establish elemental substitution as a route to thermally quenched metastability in metal-insulator transitions, expanding metastable phase control to electronic phases.

</details>


### [7] [Interlayer-engineering of Charge Order Wave Vector in Kagome Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26751)
*Muntafa M. Mahi,Quazi D. M. Khosru,M. Zahid Hasan,Mahbub Alam,Md Shafayat Hossain*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算，研究了CsV₃Sb₅中层间耦合对电荷序波矢的影响，发现单层极限下主导晶格不稳定性在q=(1/4,0,0)对应4×1调制，而随着层间耦合增强，M点声子逐渐软化并最终形成体相的2×2不稳定性，从而将层间耦合确立为连接竞争性电荷序波矢的控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome金属AV₃Sb₅中电荷序波矢的基本归属问题尚未解决，尤其是体相的2×2电荷序与主要由表面探测到的4×1调制的微观起源及其关系存在争议，需要统一的理论框架来解释不同电荷序波矢的共存与竞争。

Method: 利用第一性原理计算，通过调节层间距从单层极限到体相极限，系统研究CsV₃Sb₅中层间耦合对声子谱和晶格不稳定性的影响，分析不同波矢处声子软化的演化行为。

Result: 单层AV₃Sb₅（A=Rb, Cs）在M点无声子不稳定性，主导晶格不稳定性位于q=(1/4,0,0)，支持4×1调制；在CsV₃Sb₅中，随着层间耦合增强，M点声子逐渐软化，在c≈12.24 Å附近已变得不稳定，并演化为体相中强烈的2×2不稳定性。

Conclusion: 层间耦合是在固定化学计量比下连接竞争性4×1和2×2电荷序倾向的控制参数，为理解kagome金属中多种电荷序波矢共存与竞争提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Charge orders in the kagome metals AV$_3$Sb$_5$ sit at the center of a rich phase diagram that also includes superconductivity, nematicity, and signatures of time-reversal-symmetry breaking. Yet even the basic question of which charge ordering wave vectors are intrinsic, and which are selected by dimensionality and lattice coupling, remains unsettled. Importantly, the microscopic origin of different charge orders and, in particular, the relationship between the robust bulk $2 \times 2$ charge order and the controversial $4 \times 1$ modulation, which is primarily resolved by surface probes, remains unresolved. Here, we use first-principles calculations to study the role of interlayer coupling in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ by tuning the interlayer separation from the monolayer limit to the bulk limit. In the monolayer AV$_3$Sb$_5$ (A = Rb, Cs), the phonon spectrum exhibits no instability at the M point; instead, the dominant lattice instability occurs at q = (1/4, 0, 0), consistent with a $4 \times 1$ modulation. Abstract: Exactly solvable quantum many-body models are rare, and even when their spectra are algebraically organized, dynamical responses generally remain difficult to obtain because they probe an extensive number of excited states. Here we show that quantum geometric nesting (QGN) models admit an unusually strong form of solvability rooted in \emph{Fock-space fragmentation}: excitations on top of the exact frustration-free ground states decouple into Krylov subspaces with a fixed number of particle and hole operators, and hence remain dynamically invariant. Exploiting this structure, we prove that the stiffness of the spontaneously broken continuous symmetry in QGN models is exactly equal to its variational value in the Gaussian manifold, confirming a conjecture from quantum many-body bootstrap~\cite{GaoHanKhalaf2026}. The proof shows that an infinitesimal phase twist couples the ground state only to the one-particle, one-hole fragment, which coincides with the tangent space of the ground state within the variational manifold, thereby making the variational curvature exact. More generally, perturbations whose action remains within a fixed Fock-space fragment have response functions determined exactly by the corresponding few-body sector, enabling exact access to quantities including static susceptibility, optical conductivity, dynamical structure factors, and single-particle Green's functions.

</details>

Abstract: Continuous quantum phase transitions in interacting many-body systems exhibit universal phenomena that are largely independent of microscopic details. An organizing principle distilled from canonical examples such as the magnetic transition in Ising models is that the low-energy physics of transitions between gapped phases is governed by scale-invariant quantum field theories with gapless degrees of freedom at isolated points in momentum space. In this paper, we show that this principle can fail in a radical way: an entire gapless Fermi surface can emerge at a quantum critical point separating two fully gapped insulators. These exotic phase transitions are dubbed \textit{metallogenic quantum critical points} (MGQCP). We construct a controlled family of effective field theories for MGQCPs between distinct fractional Chern insulators and propose a periodically modulated quantum Hall bilayer as a minimal microscopic setting. The critical theory exhibits an unusual mixture of physical signatures: while transport and compressibility follow scaling laws compatible with a conformal field theory, the electron spectral function develops a power-law non-analyticity at the Fermi momentum, characteristic of non-Fermi-liquid metals. We discuss how the concept of MGQCP may shed new light on critical phenomena in moire materials with tunable Chern bands. More generally, our results point to a broader class of quantum critical phenomena in which extended manifolds of gapless excitations emerge in momentum space despite being absent in both neighboring phases.

Abstract: Magic-angle helical trilayer graphene---three graphene layers sequentially twisted in the same direction by $\sim1.8^\circ$---relaxes into a mosaic of domains that, at zero field, carry opposite valley-resolved Chern numbers, with boundaries hosting a network of gapless conducting modes. Contrary to this expectation, we observe a field-induced Chern gap with Chern number $C=-6$ emanating from charge neutrality; in this gap, the Hall resistance is quantized to within $2\%$ of the expected value, $-h/6e^2$, at 4.6 K. We explain this behavior using both Hofstadter and orbital Zeeman calculations, which show that a moderate magnetic field drives a valley-selective topological transition. Above the transition, the total Chern number of the occupied states in each spin-valley flavor becomes identical across neighboring domains, and the domain-wall modes can become gapped. Though the central valence-band Chern numbers still differ between the two domain types, the observed quantized transport attests to a global gap.

Our results highlight the experimental importance of the leads-QD and bias voltage asymmetry parameters, which are often present in realistic experimental setups, and can strongly affect the identification and observation of MBSs transport signatures.

Here, we propose a flux-tunable four-terminal normal metal-superconductor junction that achieves deterministic, 100\% efficient CAR. We demonstrate that at exactly half a magnetic flux quantum ($φ=π$), exact destructive Aharonov-Bohm and Peierls interferences structurally forbid ET and AR respectively. By tuning the central junction hopping, electron reflection is also suppressed to zero. Using the Cauchy argument principle, we prove that this suppression manifests as a quantized topological winding number, guaranteeing a topologically protected unit CAR probability. We establish that this regime is characterized by a strictly positive cross-correlation shot noise, providing an unambiguous experimental signature of Cooper-pair splitting. Furthermore, this perfect CAR is nearly broadband within the superconducting gap and remarkably robust against structural disorder, offering a highly resilient architecture for deterministic nonlocal entanglement generation.

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are non--Hermitian degeneracies at which both eigenvalues and eigenvectors coalesce, usually engineered through balanced gain and loss or non--reciprocity. In this work, we show that the radial electrostatic problem of an anisotropic core--shell nanoparticle realizes an EP without any of the alluded conditons. Written in the logarithmic radial coordinate, the problem is a Cauchy--Euler equation that maps onto a damped oscillator and, equivalently, onto a two-site Hatano--Nelson Hamiltonian, so that the non-Hermiticity is emulated in space rather than in time and the sole control parameter is the degree of dielectric anisotropy, $\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon_r$. The shell is passive, reciprocal, and lossless, yet it hosts an EP and a non--Hermitian phase transition governed by hyperbolicity alone. Beyond a hyperbolicity threshold the electric field inside the shell turns from monotonic decay into spatial oscillation, producing hotspots at intermediate radii which can be exploited to tailor fluorescence, strong coupling, sensing, or cloaking. More broadly, this establishes hyperbolic media as a platform to simulate non--Hermitian physics without gain, loss, or non--reciprocity.

Abstract: In ultra-clean conductors, fast inter-particle collisions can lead to the formation of a viscous electron fluid and realization of the hydrodynamic transport regime. Here we develop a theory of hydrodynamic magnetotransport of two-dimensional (2D) electrons in samples with low densities of defects and edges of arbitrary roughness. Within our model the roughness is described by a single parameter with the dimension of speed in the boundary conditions on sample edges. The contribution to the Hall resistance associated with the relaxation processes exhibits a saturation in the limit of high magnetic field and a minimum as a function of the magnetic field for sufficiently rough edges. The minimum disappears as the edge roughness decreases or the sample width and bulk scattering by defects increase. These properties of the Hall resistance can serve as the signs to identify the hydrodynamic regime of electron transport in experiments and can be used to determine its parameters, in particular, the degree of edge roughness.

Our results, together with previous observations of the daughter states, show that the even-denominator FQH states in bilayer graphene are compatible with a non-Abelian ground state, and that their quasiparticles can be localized around a quantum Hall antidot, a necessary ingredient for topological quantum computation.

Abstract: Majorana zero modes (MZMs) are spatially separated, near-zero-energy excitations expected at the ends of a topological superconducting (TS) wire. A quantum-dot interferometer can be used to probe the quantum capacitance of the TS wire, and the location and magnitude of the capacitance resonances provide information about the MZMs, while their magnetic flux dependence probes coherent coupling to the two Majorana modes. It has been recently shown that, a gapless (i.e., $Δ=0$) wire segment can also exhibit flux-dependent quantum capacitance oscillations through Aharonov-Bohm interference and, with suitable tuning, can reproduce a Majorana-like response. Here we show that the two mechanisms can be distinguished experimentally. In the gapless normal wire segment, the two parity-dependent signals originate from separate energy resonances corresponding to the lack of generic zero-energy states. By contrast, in the topological wire, the pair of low energy levels with even and odd parity are nearly degenerate in energy, and therefore, the two parity branches remain within the same broader resonance region in the quantum-dot potential, and persist under independent variations of the dot potential and wire chemical potential. Our results show that the experimentally observed quantum capacitance response is distinguished not by a particular flux trace at one optimized point in parameter space, but by its persistence over a finite range of independently controlled parameters, including the quantum-dot potential. This parameter space stability provides a direct means of ruling out the gapless normal wire segment as the origin of the observed Majorana-like response.

### [35] [Dynamical phase selection controls compute scaling in looped transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26556)

Abstract: A looped transformer performs inference by iterating a weight-tied map, making its computation a dynamical process whose cost is set by the resulting inference dynamics. For a Wigner matrix, the correlation length diverges at criticality but the susceptibility remains finite, and one-loop replica analysis supports an upper critical dimension of unity. In an exactly solvable spherical variant, the heat capacity jumps at the transition in $d>1$, and correlations remain pinned to their critical form throughout the glass phase, giving generic scale invariance despite the absence of a Goldstone mode.

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于最大熵和平均场近似的概率信息论框架来解决旅行商问题（TSP），通过可微的环惩罚项抑制子环，得到一个自洽的数值算法，能输出候选路径及编码竞争边的概率矩阵，在TSPLIB实例上以多项式时间匹配大部分最优解。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在合成数据集和TSPLIB实例上测试，算法在多项式时间内收敛到连通路径，在大多数实例中匹配已知最优解，其余实例相对差距较小。同时算法输出概率矩阵以编码竞争边与退化解。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) asks for the shortest route to visit a set of cities exactly once. It combines a simple local rule - each city must be visited once - with a hard, global constraint- all cities must be traversed within a single cycle. We cast the problem within a probabilistic, information-theoretic framework. The coexistence of local and global constraints is precisely what makes the problem difficult to address in this framework: the local rule can be enforced through vertex-level constraints, whereas the global constraint cannot be captured by independent edge probabilities. We show that this obstacle can be overcome by defining a maximum-entropy probability distribution over graphs, in which edge costs and degree constraints generate an assignment-like ensemble, and a global term, describing the hard constraint, tilts this ensemble toward Hamiltonian cycles. To make the construction tractable, we derive a mean-field approximation in terms of edge occupancies and implement a differentiable cycle penalty that suppresses sub-tours. This leads to a self-consistent numerical procedure whose output is not only a candidate tour but also a probability matrix encoding competing edges and degenerate solutions. We test the method on synthetic ensembles and on TSPLIB instances. The algorithm converges to connected tours in polynomial time, matching the best-known solution in the majority of instances and remaining within a small relative gap otherwise. Beyond its competitive performance, the proposed framework offers a general approach for handling hard constraints while reducing hard combinatorial optimization problems to simpler ones.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究涨落-响应关系（FDR）是从粗粒化随机动力学本身涌现的普适性质，还是需要依赖系统的额外信息（如噪声强度或微观系综细节）。这有助于厘清非平衡稳态中波动响应关系的适用边界和理论基础。

Result: 发现一个Z₂对称变换能实现时间反演并改变作用量仅一个边界项，该边界项对应稳态构型间跃迁的熵。由该对称性推出的Ward恒等式完全来自粗粒化随机理论，不依赖于噪声强度。在施加爱因斯坦关系后，这些恒等式与微观Gibbs系综在旋转Kubo-Martin-Schwinger条件下推导的高温FDR一致。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the extent to which fluctuation--response relations emerge from coarse-grained stochastic dynamics alone, and which aspects instead depend on additional information about the system. To address this question, we study the rotating stochastic Landau model, an exactly solvable system describing an overdamped charged Brownian particle in a constant magnetic field, coupled dissipatively to a rotating environment, whose steady state supports circulating probability currents. Using the Martin--Siggia--Rose path integral, we show that there is an emergent $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry transformation that implements the time-reversed dynamics and changes the action by a boundary term. Comparison with the Crooks fluctuation theorem identifies this term with the entropy associated with transitions between steady-state configurations. After coupling the theory to external sources, the same symmetry yields Ward identities relating fluctuations and response. These identities follow entirely from the coarse-grained stochastic theory and do not fix the noise strength. Finally, upon imposing the Einstein relation, we show that they coincide with the high-temperature fluctuation--dissipation relations implied by the rotating Kubo--Martin--Schwinger condition for a microscopic Gibbs ensemble.

</details>


### [41] [When dissipative steady states admit thermodynamic occupation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26621)
*Tetsu Ichitsubo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种扇区分离几何构造，通过熵产生暴露-分离方案和自主重分布，解决了非平衡稳态（NESS）中有限稳态循环与全局精确速率比场的不兼容性，从而在保持耗散的同时恢复热力学占据定律（如倒费米-狄拉克分布），并应用于量子点激光器的热涂布效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡稳态通常缺乏热力学占据定律，因为有限稳态循环流与全局精确的速率比场（rate-ratio field）无法在同一马尔可夫生成器中兼容共存。这一根本性不兼容性阻碍了在耗散非平衡系统中建立类似平衡态占据分布的热力学规律。

Method: 构造扇区分离几何以克服不兼容性，且不停止耗散。具体包括：(1) 熵产生暴露-分离方案：将熵产生状态0从条件占据流形中排除，同时保留在耗散全图中；(2) 在强偏置/快速重置（SR）极限下，物理回返路径i→0→0*变为有效马尔可夫过程；(3) 对于热力学完整的条件流形，通过自主重分布（AR）消除残余无效循环，使速率比单形式精确；(4) 热力学标定给出占据概率形式p_i=e^{X_i}/Z_C，全图概率精确因子化为P_α=(1-P_0)p_α。

Result: 在SR极限下，动力学因子趋近于1，p_α→e^{X_α}/Z_C，全图概率P_α→p_α而有限耗散持续存在。在AR附近，可积性以残余循环电流的线性阶失去，而耗散以二次阶开始。在二元零循环秩极限下，占据在保持Δμ偏置下自主重分布，产生倒费米-狄拉克定律，并应用于量子点激光器的热涂布。框架提供了耗散NESS中热力学占据定律的建构性获取条件和失效诊断。

Conclusion: 该工作首次揭示了在保持有限耗散的前提下，非平衡稳态中的热力学占据定律可以精确成立，前提是通过扇区分离和自主重分布机制消除循环流与精确速率比场之间的根本冲突。所得倒费米-狄拉克分布在量子点激光器中具有实际应用价值，且框架提供了系统的获取条件与失败诊断工具。

Abstract: Non-equilibrium steady states (NESSs) generally lack thermodynamic occupation laws because finite stationary circulation and a globally exact rate-ratio field cannot coexist for the same Markov generator. Here we construct a sector-separated geometry that overcomes this incompatibility without arresting dissipation. Entropy-production exposure-and-separation excludes the entropy-producing state~$0$ from the conditional occupation manifold while retaining it in the dissipative full graph; physical returns $i\to0\to0^\ast$ become effectively Markovian in the strong-bias/rapid-reset (SR) limit. For a thermodynamically complete conditional manifold, autonomous redistribution (AR) eliminates residual futile circulation, making the rate-ratio one-form exact. Thermodynamic calibration gives $X_i=β(Δμ- \mathcal F_i^{\mathrm{cost}})$ and $p_i=e^{X_i}/Z_\mathcal{C}$, with $Z_\mathcal{C}=1+\sum_i e^{X_i}$. Full-graph probabilities factorize exactly as $P_α=(1-P_0)p_α$. In the SR limit, the kinetic factor tends to unity while $p_α\to e^{X_α}/Z_\mathcal C$, yielding $P_α\to p_α$ while finite dissipation persists. Near AR, integrability is lost linearly in residual cycle current whereas dissipation begins quadratically. In the binary zero-cycle-rank limit, occupation redistributes autonomously under maintained $Δμ$ bias, yielding the inverted Fermi--Dirac law, which is applied to thermal smearing in quantum-dot lasers. The framework provides constructive acquisition conditions and failure diagnostics for thermodynamic occupation laws in dissipative NESSs.

</details>


### [42] [Brownian yet non-Gaussian diffusion through equilibrium nonlinear friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26773)
*Jakob Mihatsch,Andreas M. Menzel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在正确的随机平衡条件下，非线性摩擦可以解释均匀环境中的布朗但非高斯扩散（BnGD）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Counting processes provide a fundamental description of stochastic events ranging from photon detection to clock ticks. A central question is how accurately such events can be timed when only finite memory resources are available. Here, we investigate this problem within a general framework of finite-dimensional classical and quantum counting processes. We derive a rigorous finite-memory variance bound obeyed by every classical $d$-state counting process, which is tight and saturated by a discrete Erlang-type ladder process. Through numerical optimization, we identify quantum counting processes that violate this classical bound, achieving smaller first-tick fluctuations than any classical process with the same memory size and mean tick time. For the qubit case, we further derive an analytical large-mean bound within a single-Kraus no-tick family, showing that the quantum advantage persists asymptotically within this class. The optimized quantum processes exhibit coherent conditioned dynamics and approach a continuous-time quantum-jump description as the mean increases. Our results establish a finite-memory quantum advantage in temporal precision and connect discrete-time counting processes with continuous-time quantum timekeeping.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov和Lanczos逼近广泛应用于量子动力学、量子子空间方法和哈密顿量学习中，但一个核心的实际问题是：一个m维Krylov截断在多大时间尺度内可以被信任？此前缺乏严格的理论框架来刻画这一"断裂时间"，因此需要建立基于因果传播原理的严格界。

Method: 本文提出在相关的Jacobi链上研究因果传播，关键创新在于使用非均匀传输度量ρ(m,n) = Σ_{j=min(m,n)}^{max(m,n)-1} 1/b_j（其中b_j为Lanczos跳跃振幅）代替传统的Krylov指标作为距离度量。作者在该度量下证明了Lieb-Robinson界，其小权重极限给出速度v_LR = 2，意味着在锥ρ(m,n) ≈ 2|t|外传播呈指数抑制。通过将Lieb-Robinson界与Duhamel公式结合，推导出截断动力学误差的下界，进而得到断裂时间的下界t*(m;ε) ≥ τ_m[1-o(1)]。

Result: 证明了Lieb-Robinson界在非均匀传输度量下成立，速度v_LR = 2。断裂时间的下界为τ_m = ρ(0,m)，在探针沿链传播时该界是tight的。更重要的是，当探针只激发谱的局部或已被截断分辨的部分时，存在状态依赖的临界Krylov维度m*，超过该维度后截断误差可以始终很小，断裂时间趋于无穷大。数值实验（自旋链和随机Jacobi矩阵）支持这些理论预测。

Conclusion: Krylov截断的可信时间由Jacobi链上的因果传播决定，而非Krylov指标本身。在传输受限情形下断裂时间约为τ_m；但在谱局部激发的情形下，截断可以在任意长时间内保持有效。这一理论为实际使用Krylov方法的时间和维度选择提供了严格的指导原则。

Abstract: Krylov and Lanczos approximations are used in quantum dynamics, quantum subspace methods, and Hamiltonian learning. A practical question is how long an $m$-dimensional Krylov truncation can be trusted. We argue that this time is fixed by causal propagation on the associated Jacobi chain. The relevant distance is not the Krylov index itself, but the inhomogeneous transport metric $ρ(m,n) = \sum_{j=\min(m,n)}^{\max(m,n)-1} 1/b_j$, where $b_j$ is the Lanczos hopping across the bond $j \leftrightarrow j+1$. We prove a Lieb--Robinson bound in this metric. Its small-weight limit gives the velocity $v_{\rm LR} = 2$, meaning that propagation is exponentially suppressed outside the cone $ρ(m,n) \simeq 2|t|$. The error of a finite Krylov approximation to the return amplitude is a round-trip effect: information has to travel from the probe to the truncation boundary and back. Combining the Lieb--Robinson bound with Duhamel's formula yields a lower bound on the error of the truncated dynamics. For a fixed tolerance $ε$, let the break time $t_\ast(m;ε)$ denote the longest time for which the $m$-dimensional truncation is guaranteed to reproduce the exact return amplitude within error $ε$. We show that $t_\ast(m;ε) \ge τ_m[1-o(1)]$, where $τ_m = ρ(0,m) = \sum_{j<m} 1/b_j$. When the probe spreads along the chain, this lower bound is also tight, so $t_\ast(m) \simeq τ_m$. The situation is different when the probe excites only a localized part of the spectrum, or a part already resolved by the truncation. In this case, essentially no signal reaches the boundary. Beyond a state-dependent Krylov dimension $m_\ast$, the approximation can therefore remain accurate at all times, and the break time is effectively infinite. Numerical tests on spin chains and random Jacobi matrices support $t_\ast(m) \simeq τ_m$ in the transport-limited regime.

</details>

Abstract: The physics of information scrambling in quantum many-body systems is intimately related to thermalisation and emergence of chaos. However, its standard characterisation through bipartite entanglement or operator growth remains inherently coarse-grained, obscuring the spatiotemporal anatomy of how quantum information flows between different regions of the system and across various length scales. In this work, we develop a theory for the dynamics of local information in local random unitary circuits which resolves the fine-grained microscopic structures of local information flow in such systems. Using the framework of information lattice which systematises the information content at each length scale within each subsystem, we identify a distribution of length scales at which information resides and obtain the dynamics of the distribution. For Haar-random circuits with infinite local Hilbert-space dimensions, we map this dynamics onto an exact classical stochastic process which reveals that this distribution is described by a Tracy-Widom form which moves ballistically in time ($ \propto t$) toward larger scales, accompanied by a $\sim t^{1/3}$ broadening. We also find the same qualitative behaviour for random Clifford circuits acting on qubits. The similar scaling behaviour in two rather different settings hints strongly towards the universality of our results. In the case of Clifford circuits, we develop a phenomenological Markov process for the dynamics of the stabiliser generators in a specific gauge which confirms the Tracy-Widom distribution and the $t^{1/3}$ scaling of the fluctuations. Ultimately, our results establish the universal properties of the dynamics of local quantum information in a length scale-resolved fashion and provide a possible route towards bridging exact microscopic theories for information dynamics with emergent hydrodynamic descriptions of information flow.

We further introduce the twisted Renyi-$N$ correlator (TRNC) as a probe of temporal correlations in the channel and demonstrate that it is dual to the mSPT strange correlator, providing a direct bulk-boundary route to diagnose long-range temporal order implied by the LSM anomaly. Abstract: The Koopman-von Neumann framework has been proposed to design quantum algorithms for non-linear dynamics. On the conceptual side, relativistic collapse theories have been claimed to face challenges involving tension between instantaneous collapse and relativity, frame-dependence of property values and probabilities, the possibility of superluminal signaling and the failure of narratability. Abstract: Understanding the temporal and spatial dependence of temperature is critical in high-performance cryogenic devices. Typical thermometry techniques struggle to simultaneously combine high bandwidth, sensitivity and on-chip integration, limiting their ability to measure fast intra-device thermal fluctuations. Here, we demonstrate a time-resolved thermometry platform based on TiN kinetic inductance resonators integrated in a 22 nm FDSOI chip. By tracking temperature-dependent shifts in the resonant frequency, we achieve sub-millikelvin temperature sensitivity down to temperatures of 100 mK. Time-resolved on-chip pulsed heating experiments as a function of distance reveal an onset delay, consistent with a quasi-ballistic heat propagation velocity of 3.9 ${\pm}$ 0.1 mm $μ$s${}^{-1}$. We also show that elevated temperatures increase net thermal conductance, shortening thermal relaxation times across all spatial separations. This behaviour manifests in two distinct regimes: a substrate-limited regime at 100 mK, where cooling rates vary with heater distance, and a Kapitza boundary-limited regime at 400 mK, where thermal relaxation becomes more spatially uniform.These measurements demonstrate kinetic inductance thermometry's ability to rapidly probe non-equilibrium temperature dynamics in cryogenic devices such as quantum processors.

Abstract: Remote charging of a quantum battery (QB) is hindered by radiative leakage of the excitation into the photonic environment that acts as a mediator for energy transfer. We consider a charger-battery model consisting of two two-level systems (TLSs) that are locally coupled to two sites of a one-dimensional coupled cavity array. When their transition frequency lies outside the propagation band, the system forms atom-photon bound states with localized photonic components, and the overlap of these components lifts the degeneracy of the even- and odd-parity bound states, yielding an energy splitting that drives coherent energy transfer from the charger to the QB. In this way, the band gap suppresses resonant emission and the localized bound states mediate remote charging. From the parity-resolved spectrum, we relate the charging time to the energy splitting and the charged ergotropy to the fraction of TLS population on the bound states. Bound states closer to the band edge extend the interaction range of the TLSs but contain a large photonic fraction and are consequently more susceptible to photon loss.

Abstract: The realization of large-scale quantum networks requires more than advances in quantum repeaters, memories, and processors, it requires a framework explaining how heterogeneous quantum technologies evolve from isolated deployments into interconnected infrastructures. While the classical Internet evolved under strong utility incentives associated with resource sharing and communication demands, quantum networking currently lacks dominant applications capable of generating comparable incentives. As a consequence, contemporary quantum networks are largely formed through technology-driven decisions motivated by technical feasibility, experimental validation, and expected future value. This work argues that the absence of utility-driven network formation is not solely a consequence of immature applications, but also of insufficient abstraction. In particular, heterogeneous quantum platforms remain tightly coupled to the functionalities they provide, preventing the definition of technology-independent utility functions. A hierarchical architecture consisting of Physical Platforms (PP), Functionalities (F), Services (S), Applications (A), and Use-Cases (UC) is proposed, together with the argument that quantum interconnects constitute the enabling technology required to decouple physical implementations from network functionalities. Such decoupling permits the definition of utility functions at the functionality level and establishes the conditions under which strategic (agent-based) network formation becomes applicable. Quantum interconnects should therefore be viewed not only as interoperability devices, but also as fundamental enablers of strategic quantum network evolution.

Abstract: We revisit a degree-only arc Hamiltonian for fixed-fleet, homogeneous, uncapacitated vehicle routing. Because its local penalties define only a cycle cover, ground states may contain customer cycles disconnected from the depot. We construct a polynomial-size quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) repair using capped single-commodity flow and prove that every ground-state routing is connected and cost-optimal under explicit penalty assumptions. For $N-1$ customers and $K$ nonempty routes, the unreduced encoding uses exactly $|E|(1+\lceil\log_2(N-K+1)\rceil)$ logical problem qubits. A reversible compute--phase--uncompute realization evaluates the flow penalties in $O(N^2\log N+N\log^2N)$ logical gates on a complete graph with $O(\log N)$ reusable workspace and no product register. On complete loopless graphs, a depot-delimited single-sequence position encoding uses fewer problem qubits and fewer written terms when the flow-word length grows. Conversely, the flow model achieves a smaller structured logical-gate upper bound under a common reversible accounting model. Exact audits of the Hamiltonian and circuit implementation, combined with a $1{,}200$-matrix classical benchmark, verify the formulation and quantify the connectivity gap. Finally, a 32,000-shot Amazon Braket task on IQM Emerald characterizes depth-one termwise Ising circuits on a diagnostic $N = 4,\, K = 1$ counterexample instance. In the degree-only circuit, $78.05\%$ of selected $p=1$ shots realize the invalid disconnected ground state; the reduced 14-qubit flow-augmented circuit yields no fully feasible sample. These device results characterize mapped Hamiltonians and compilation rather than an asymptotic routing solution advantage.

Abstract: We investigate the effect of the GUP on the Lamb shift of a two-level atom interacting with a real massless scalar quantum field, within the DDC formalism. For an atom undergoing inertial motion, uniform acceleration, and uniform circular motion, we analyze the separate contributions of vacuum fluctuations and radiation reaction. We first derive the statistical functions of the field along the atom's trajectories for the three types of motion, expressing them as frequency integrals, and then employ them to calculate the vacuum fluctuation and radiation reaction contributions to the radiative level shift. We show that the GUP-modified Lamb shift of the two-level atom arises entirely from vacuum fluctuations and acquires additional corrections proportional to $β$. We focus in particular on the acceleration-dependent GUP corrections. For a uniformly accelerated atom, the GUP corrections comprise thermal and nonthermal parts. At low accelerations, the thermal part exhibits nonmonotonic behavior, and is proportional to $a^4$ in the limit $a/ω_0 \to 0$; the nonthermal part, by contrast, grows nonlinearly and monotonically, exceeding the thermal part by nearly two orders of magnitude at large accelerations. For an atom in uniform circular motion, the GUP corrections are purely nonthermal and also display a nonlinear, monotonic dependence on acceleration, increasing or decreasing steeply according to the sign of $β$. For the same $β$, the corrections are larger in uniform circular motion than in uniformly accelerated motion, since the former involves terms proportional to both $a^2$ and $a^3$, whereas the latter contains only $a^2$ terms.

Abstract: High-frequency squeezed states are important for quantum metrology and information processing, but quadrature measurements at gigahertz frequencies remain challenging. Balanced homodyne detection (BHD), the standard approach, requires closely matched complex transfer functions in both photodetection channels to suppress local-oscillator (LO) noise and maintain a well-defined readout quadrature. This matching becomes increasingly difficult at high bandwidths. Strongly asymmetric single-photodiode homodyne detection avoids this requirement, but achieving high signal efficiency and shot-noise clearance typically requires hundreds of milliwatts of LO power and does not suppress technical LO sidebands. I propose cavity-assisted homodyne detection, in which the quantum field and LO enter separate ports of an impedance-matched traveling-wave cavity. The resonant LO carrier is transmitted to the photodetector, where it beats with off-resonant signal sidebands reflected from the cavity. At the same time, technical LO sidebands outside the cavity linewidth are suppressed. I derive the quantum input-output relations and linearized photocurrent including intracavity loss, and show that near-unity signal-transfer efficiency can be achieved at moderate incident LO power. The cavity linewidth and free spectral range determine the usable detection band.

Abstract: Ghost imaging enables nonlocal image reconstruction and exhibits strong robustness against interference, but achieving high-fidelity recognition at ultra-low sampling rates remains challenging. This method also exhibits strong robustness to quantum noise and has been validated on a real optical ghost imaging system, achieving an average recognition accuracy of 84.8%. Abstract: Distributed Quantum Computing (DQC) addresses the physical scaling limitations of monolithic quantum processors by networking modular Quantum Processing Units (QPUs). Efficient execution of quantum algorithms on DQC architectures requires compiling them across QPUs while minimizing inter-QPU communication bottlenecks, primarily through circuit partitioning. However, current evaluations of state-of-the-art partitioning heuristics focus primarily on the total entanglement cost of the partitions, failing to capture the broader structural and temporal overheads introduced by distributed network constraints. This paper addresses this evaluation gap by applying established monolithic benchmarking metrics to partitioned distributed circuits to quantify the performance impact of network constraints. Using an open-source, automated evaluation pipeline, we systematically assess diverse partitioning algorithms across standardized workloads and quantum network topologies. Our empirical results reveal that partitioning algorithms with comparable entanglement costs can still introduce drastically different physical execution penalties. By exposing these hidden trade-offs, such as severe increases in circuit depth and substantial reductions in gate density, this study demonstrates that comprehensive circuit-level metrics are essential for guiding the future design of DQC compilers.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究提出多电子Dirac-Coulomb哈密顿量的单电子时间反演平方和可作为具有整数本征值谱的守恒量，及其期望值被用作Kramers污染诊断。本文旨在弄清该构造背后的真实算子身份并检验其守恒性主张。

Conclusion: 该文献中提出的量子数及其作为Kramers污染诊断工具的合理性被推翻：伪自旋分量与哈密顿量不对易，其本征值非守恒量，且依赖基组相位选择而无物理意义。

Abstract: The square of a sum of single-electron time reversals has been proposed as a conserved quantity with an integer eigenvalue spectrum for the many-electron Dirac-Coulomb Hamiltonian [Phys. Rev. A 94, 052104 (2016)], and its expectation value is in use as a diagnostic of Kramers contamination. This work identifies the operator behind the construction, a component of a pseudospin attached to the chosen Kramers pairs, whose angular-momentum algebra reproduces the reported spectrum and yields the eigenvectors in closed form. The label $k$ entering the proposed quantum number $-k^2$ is twice the magnitude of a pseudospin projection onto an axis fixed by the basis and changes when the Kramers pairs are rephased. The commutation with the Hamiltonian is unfounded, and the proposed quantum number does not stand.

</details>

Abstract: Private distributed quantum sensing aims to estimate an authorized collective parameter while preventing independent estimation of individual local parameters. Here, we analytically characterize Gaussian quantum networks satisfying this perfect local privacy condition. Using a graph representation of the Gaussian pairing matrix, we show that two-mode squeezed vacuum states constitute the essential building blocks of privacy-preserving Gaussian states. We then analytically derive the optimal sensitivity under perfect local privacy and determine a Gaussian probe that achieves Heisenberg scaling with respect to both the photon number and the number of sensing modes. Using local homodyne measurements and maximum-likelihood estimation, we numerically verify quantum-enhanced sensitivity beyond the shot-noise limit while preserving perfect local privacy. We further show that the local-privacy condition is preserved under arbitrary phase-independent quantum channels, including optical loss. Our results provide a general framework for constructing and optimizing privacy-preserving continuous-variable quantum sensing networks.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作提供了系统研究非简谐环境对开放量子系统影响的数值精确工具，揭示了非简谐环境区别于简谐近似的重要物理现象，为理解更真实环境条件下量子系统的行为开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In open quantum systems, the environment is typically modelled as a collection of harmonic oscillators, whereas realistic environments often exhibit unique non-Markovian effects due to the anharmonicity of the environment. Here we present a framework for modelling anharmonic environments in open quantum systems numerically exactly, in which, via a matching criterion, we map the anharmonic environment onto an effective harmonic one. This results in an effective spectral density that is a collection of shifted bare spectra arising from the non-equidistant energy gaps of the anharmonic environment, along with a unique zero-frequency term that effectively acts as static disorder on the system. This is due to the non-zero variance of the diagonal of the bath coupling operator. In tandem, we develop a framework for investigating the effects of environments comprising a few damped anharmonic modes where the matching criterion fails to work due to the non-Gaussianity of the environment. For both continuous and discrete damped anharmonic environments, this work investigates their unique influence on the system's dynamics, including enhanced non-Markovianity compared to a harmonic approximation, novel effects in the absorption spectrum, and the potential for anharmonic environments to enhance energy transport.

</details>

Abstract: Contraction coefficients quantify the loss of distinguishability induced by a channel and provide a strong form of the data-processing inequality. While standard contraction coefficients ignore auxiliary quantum systems, existing extensions based on complete contraction coefficients require the compared states to have identical reference marginals. In this work, we introduce conditional contraction coefficients, a novel family that incorporates arbitrary quantum reference systems by subtracting the distinguishability already present in the reference system. We develop a general framework for contraction coefficients with such quantum side information, including the corresponding strong-data-processing-inequality (SDPI) constants, expansion coefficients, and relative contraction coefficients. For the trace distance, we show that the optimization can be restricted to orthogonal input states. For the quantum relative entropy, we prove that its conditional contraction coefficient is exactly equal to the contraction coefficient of the conditional mutual information, extending the classical correspondence between relative-entropy contraction and mutual-information contraction to the setting with quantum side information. More generally, we identify structural properties of divergences required for these results and discuss extensions beyond the relative entropy. These results establish a unified framework for analyzing information contraction in quantum network settings, where quantum side information and distributed correlations are intrinsic features of the information-processing task. Applications include an extension of the Polyanskiy-Wu bounds on mutual information contraction, new perspectives on mixing times, and fundamental limits on quantum memories.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态合并（quantum state merging）是量子信息论中最基本的通信任务之一，但此前对其强逆指数（strong converse exponent）的刻画尚不清楚。理解这一指数对于判定量子态合并在该错误率区域的最优纠缠消耗速度至关重要，并有助于揭示该任务与其他量子信息协议之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过建立量子态合并强逆指数与优化后α-z条件Rényi熵（取z=α/2）之间的联系，利用条件Rényi熵族的一般框架进行分析，并分别针对部分平滑（partial smoothing）和全局平滑（global smoothing）两种情形推导出相应的强逆指数表达式。

Result: 量子态合并的强逆指数由z=α/2的优化α-z条件Rényi熵刻画，而非通常的夹层条件Rényi熵。在此基础上，推导了纯化距离下部分平滑条件最小熵的强逆指数，该指数由club-sandwiched条件熵控制；而若采用全局平滑，则得到夹层条件熵的表达形式。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了量子态合并的强逆指数具有独特的特征，不同于量子信息论中常见的夹层Rényi熵支配模式。同时，部分平滑与全局平滑之间的差异导致强逆指数由不同类别的条件Rényi熵刻画，这为理解平滑熵在不同平滑方式下的强逆行为提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We determine the strong converse exponent for the entanglement cost of quantum state merging, showing that it is characterized by the optimized $α$-$z$ conditional Rényi entropies with $z=α/2\in[1/2,1]$. This contrasts with the sandwiched conditional Rényi entropies that typically govern strong converse exponents in quantum information theory. As a consequence, we derive the strong converse exponent of the partially smoothed conditional min-entropy in purified distance. This exponent is governed by club-sandwiched conditional entropies, whereas global smoothing leads to a sandwiched expression.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干多拷贝测量能显著减少学习未知量子态性质的样本数，但实验上难以实现；现有实验通常逐拷贝制备和测量，并可能依据先前结果自适应调整后续测量。然而自适应性使测量策略空间难以刻画，PPT松弛虽可用更大且数学上易处理的测量类别绕过此复杂度，但可能削弱所得界。作者希望弄清PPT松弛在关键任务中是否真的会损失最优性。

Method: 作者针对纯度检验（purity testing）和乘积检验（product testing）两个任务，证明针对全类PPT测量的下界可被非自适应单拷贝协议紧致匹配。关键在于利用基本的对称子空间恒等式，为自适应单拷贝测量导出尖锐下界，绕过了处理自适应策略复杂性的困难。

Result: 在渐近样本复杂度层面，PPT松弛对纯度检验和乘积检验均不产生损失：全类PPT测量下界与非自适应单拷贝协议的上界匹配。证明简洁，仅需基本对称子空间恒等式即可得到自适应单拷贝测量的尖锐下界。

Conclusion: 对于纯度和乘积检验这两种基本任务，放宽到PPT测量类别并不会削弱渐近样本复杂度，非自适应单拷贝协议即可达到最优。这为分析更广泛的自适应单拷贝测量问题提供了简洁而有力的新途径。

Abstract: Coherent measurements across multiple copies of an unknown quantum state can substantially reduce the number of samples required to learn its properties, but remain experimentally challenging. Current experiments typically prepare and measure one copy at a time, potentially adapting later measurements to earlier outcomes. A central challenge is adaptivity, which makes the space of possible measurement strategies difficult to characterize. The positive-partial-transpose (PPT) relaxation bypasses this complexity by considering a larger, mathematically tractable class of measurements, at the risk of weakening the resulting bounds. Here we show, surprisingly, that the relaxation loses nothing at the level of asymptotic sample complexity for two fundamental tasks: purity testing and product testing. In both cases, lower bounds against the full class of PPT measurements are matched by nonadaptive single-copy protocols. Moreover, our proof requires only basic symmetric subspace identities, providing a simple route to sharp lower bounds for adaptive single-copy measurements.

</details>


### [95] [Efficient formation and identification of single emitters in 4H-SiC following maskless heavy ion implantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27281)
*S. Eserin,L. K. S. Zimmermann,E. Schneider,M. Ludlow,J. Heiler,O. G. Lloyd-Willard,K. Stockbridge,G. Aresta,L. Antwis,S. K. Clowes,M. Hofmann,S. Kucera,P. Berwian,F. Kaiser,B. N. Murdin*

TL;DR: 本文报道了在4H-SiC中利用低能无掩模重离子（铋和锡）注入形成隔离的亮单光子发射体，通过退火优化（900-1000°C）实现最高18%的单发射体产率，并提出分层的快速表征方案，无需耗时低温光谱即可区分发射体类型，为室温量子传感等技术提供了高效器件制造路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧空位相关的SiC单光子发射体具有极高的光学亮度和强自旋读取对比度，是规模化量子技术的有力平台；然而其形成缺乏无掩模工艺，且快速识别面临挑战，这构成了发展可扩展量子器件的关键瓶颈。

Method: 采用低能无掩模注入重离子（铋Bi和锡Sn），随后退火优化产率；用统计数据建模估算最佳注入剂量；引入分级表征方案：先用简单强度阈值筛选单发射体候选，再通过光子关联测量确认，最后利用极化、饱和计数率和磁共振频率之间的相关性对发射体类型进行分配。

Result: 退火后高达18%的注入位点承载单发射体，最优退火温度为900-1000°C；实验表明无需耗时低温光谱即可区分发射体类型；分级筛选方案实现了单发射体的快速确认与类型归属。

Conclusion: 无掩模重离子注入结合选择性筛选，为室温量子技术（如量子传感）中近表面单发射体的高效形成与快速识别提供了可行且可扩展的路线。

Abstract: Single photon emitters in silicon carbide (SiC) are a leading platform for scalable quantum technologies. Recent interest has focused on oxygen-vacancy-related emitters, which show exceptionally high optical brightness and strong spin readout contrast. One barrier to scalable quantum devices based on these emitters is the challenge of maskless formation and rapid identification. Here, we demonstrate the formation of isolated bright single emitters in 4H-SiC, using low-energy maskless implantation of heavy ions bismuth and tin. Following annealing, up to 18% of implanted sites host a single emitter, with optimal yields achieved at annealing temperatures of 900-1000 degrees C. Occupancy statistics are modelled to estimate the implantation dose that maximises single-emitter yield. We introduce a tiered characterisation scheme, where a simple intensity threshold isolates single-emitter candidates, confirmed through photon correlation measurements, after which correlations between polarisation, saturation count rate and magnetic resonance frequency assign emitter type. It is shown that time-consuming low-temperature spectroscopy is not necessary to distinguish emitter types. Together, maskless heavy-ion implantation and selective screening offer an efficient route to forming and rapidly identifying near-surface single emitters for room-temperature quantum technologies such as quantum sensing.

</details>


### [96] [Optimal cloning of mixed states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27298)
*Marco Fanizza,Dmitry Grinko,Thilo Scharnhorst,Jack Spilecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子态的近似克隆与近似转置问题，证明了对秩为r的混合态进行近似克隆需要n=Θ(krd/ε)份拷贝，这与此前只知道的上界O(krd/ε)匹配，从而确定了该问题的精确样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Werner的纯态克隆器表明克隆纯态需要n=Θ(kd/ε)份拷贝；通过随机纯化信道可将其自然扩展到混合态克隆，给出上界n=O(krd/ε)。但此前不清楚是否能做得更好，即下界是否紧，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 作者通过研究"投影仪克隆"这一特例来证明下界：输入态被承诺为P/r的形式（P为秩-r正交投影仪）。通过分析该特例的克隆难度，证明了n=Ω(krd/ε)的必要性，从而与已有的随机纯化信道方法给出的上界相匹配。同样的技术还被应用于近似转置问题。

Result: 证明了混合态近似克隆的下界为n=Ω(krd/ε)，与已知上界匹配，得到精确的样本复杂度Θ(krd/ε)。此外，将相同技术应用于近似转置问题，同样得到n=Θ(krd/ε)的精确结果。

Conclusion: 混合态近似克隆与近似转置都遵循相同的样本复杂度Θ(krd/ε)，无法通过更精巧的方案超越随机纯化信道方法的性能。这为这两个量子信息处理任务确立了最终的最优样本复杂度。

Abstract: We consider the problem of approximate cloning of quantum states: given $n$ copies of an unknown state $ρ\in \mathbb{C}^{d \times d}$, prepare an $(n+k)$-copy state with high fidelity to $ρ^{\otimes (n+k)}$. Werner's pure state cloner is the optimal channel for the pure state case, and shows that $n = Θ(kd/\varepsilon)$ copies are necessary and sufficient to clone $k$ additional copies of an unknown pure state to fidelity $1-\varepsilon$. The random purification channel gives a straightforward extension of Werner's cloner to mixed state inputs: given $n$ copies of a mixed state, randomly purify your input, apply Werner's channel in the larger Hilbert space, and then trace out the auxiliary registers. This gives a mixed state cloner using $n = O(krd/\varepsilon)$ copies to clone rank-$r$ states. Can one do any better? We show that the answer is no: one must use $n = Ω(krd/\varepsilon)$ copies. We prove our lower bound by studying the special case of projector cloning, in which the input state $ρ$ is promised to be of the form $P/r$, where $P$ is a rank-$r$ orthogonal projector.
  As a further application of our techniques, we consider the closely related problem of approximate transposition of quantum states, where one seeks to convert $ρ^{\otimes n}$ to a $k$-copy state with high fidelity to $(ρ^T)^{\otimes k}$. Here, we again show $n = Θ(krd/\varepsilon)$ copies are necessary and sufficient for this task.

</details>


### [97] [The Geometry of Dissipative Complexity: A Levi-Type Decomposition Theorem for Markovian Quantum Dynamics via Lie Wedges and Invariant Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27330)
*Yibin Wang,Dhara Thakkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了动力李楔（dynamical Lie wedge）的Levi分解与重构定理，将开马尔可夫系统中的耗散结构分解为半单与可解部分，并揭示了总耗散强度仅取决于可解根坐标这一关键性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭量子系统中控制哈密顿量由李代数描述，但开马尔可夫系统的耗散引入了不可逆方向，李代数无法捕捉这些信息。动力李楔作为局部容许生成元的凸锥保留该信息，然而其结构分解理论尚缺失，需要将经典Levi分解推广到李楔的新框架。

Method: 作者将经典有限维李代数的Levi分解定理推广至动力李楔，提出分解与重构定理：将楔分解为半单与可解数据，记录二者的耦合方式，并给出逆向重构步骤。由此推导出生成代数的四种结构类型，并分析各类型下容许生成元的耗散性质。

Result: 证明了动力李楔的分解与重构定理，得出生成代数的四种结构类型；证明每个容许生成元的总耗散强度仅依赖于其在可解根中的坐标；特别地，当生成李代数为半单时，所有容许生成元均为哈密顿量（无耗散）。

Conclusion: 该工作将李代数结构方法扩展到不可逆马尔可夫控制领域，阐明了耗散如何在生成元几何中被编码，为开放量子系统的控制理论提供了新的结构框架。

Abstract: Lie algebras describe how control Hamiltonians combine in closed quantum systems. In open Markovian systems, however, dissipation introduces irreversible directions that the Lie algebra alone does not retain. A dynamical Lie wedge preserves this information as a convex cone of locally admissible generators. The classical Levi decomposition separates a finite-dimensional Lie algebra into semisimple and solvable parts. In this work, we prove a corresponding decomposition and reconstruction theorem for dynamical Lie wedges.
  The theorem decomposes the wedge into semisimple and solvable data, records how these components are coupled, and provides a converse reconstruction of the wedge. The framework yields four structural types of the generated algebra. We further prove that the total dissipation strength of every admissible generator depends only on its coordinate in the solvable radical. In particular, if the generated Lie algebra is semisimple, every admissible generator is Hamiltonian. Together, these results extend Lie-algebraic structural methods to irreversible Markovian control and clarify how dissipation is encoded in the generator geometry.

</details>


### [98] [Evaluating Quantum Kernel Methods for Track-Based Classification in High-Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27333)
*Emmanuel Billias,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的量子核分类系统化设计，通过基于CLAS12漂移室质心特征的量子支持向量分类器（QSVC）进行粒子径迹分类。利用六量子比特ZZFeatureMap编码每个事件并构建量子核矩阵，结合MPI多节点HPC分配，成功扩展到10万训练事件和40万测试事件，比先前HEP量子核研究大一个数量级以上。理想QSVC在所有模型中取得最高召回率（99.99%），即便在硬件噪声下召回率仍保持99.51%，AUC从0.9985降至0.9671。几何分析显示量子嵌入在无噪声下呈现类间近正交、类内连贯的结构，噪声下向最大混合态压缩但保持相对排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理（HEP）中的量子核分类此前受限于小规模数据，缺乏可扩展的工作流。作者希望突破这一瓶颈，在HEP相关规模上系统评估量子核方法的可行性与实际噪声影响，为量子增强分类提供可复现的实证基准。

Method: 采用六量子比特全纠缠ZZFeatureMap将每个粒子事件编码为量子态，以量子态间的保真度构建量子核矩阵，嵌入标准SVM框架。将态制备与核构建解耦，并通过MPI多节点HPC分配分布式评估核矩阵，实现精确构造10^5训练与4×10^5测试事件的核矩阵。同时在FakeMumbaiV2校准硬件噪声模型下评估性能，并结合几何分析与经典SVM及ERT基线对比。

Result: 理想QSVC召回率达99.99%（所有模型最高）；噪声下AUC从0.9985降至0.9671，峰值显著性从17.5降至约3.5，但召回率仍保持99.51%。量子嵌入在理想环境下呈类间近正交结构，噪声下向最大混合态压缩但保持相对排序。数据规模比先前HEP量子核研究大一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了量子核分类可扩展到HEP相关数据规模，并量化了真实硬件噪声对量子增强分类的实际成本——信号保留优势虽被衰减但未完全消除。提供了可扩展、可复现的量子核实验工作流，为后续HEP量子机器学习研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We present a systematic design for large-scale quantum kernel classification, demonstrated through a quantum support vector classifier (QSVC) for particle-track classification using centroid-based CLAS12 drift-chamber features. Each event is encoded into a six-qubit state via a fully entangled ZZFeatureMap, whose fidelities define a quantum kernel within a standard SVM framework. By decoupling state preparation from kernel construction and distributing evaluation across a multi-node MPI-based HPC allocation, the approach scales to 1.0x10^5 training and 4.0x10^5 test events with an exactly constructed kernel matrix, to our knowledge more than an order of magnitude larger than prior high-energy-physics quantum-kernel studies.
  Benchmarked against linear, polynomial, RBF, and sigmoid SVM kernels and extremely randomized trees (ERT), the ideal QSVC achieves the highest recall (99.99%) among all models. Under a calibrated hardware noise model (FakeMumbaiV2, 500 training / 2,000 test events), AUC falls from 0.9985 to 0.9671 and peak significance improvement falls from 17.5 to ~3.5, yet recall remains at 99.51% -- indicating this signal-retention advantage is attenuated but not eliminated by circuit-level decoherence.
  Geometric analysis of the quantum embedding shows near-orthogonal inter-class states with coherent intra-class neighborhoods under ideal simulation; under noise this structure compresses toward the maximally mixed state while preserving its relative ordering.
  These results demonstrate a scalable, reproducible workflow for quantum kernel experimentation at HEP-relevant scale, quantifying the practical cost of realistic hardware noise on quantum-enhanced classification.

</details>


### [99] [Dynamics-based nonclassicality witness under dissipative dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27337)
*Nguyen Vu Khoi Huynh,Nicky Nel Narido Labayna,Martine Schut,Valerio Scarani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Tsirelson基于动力学的方法从孤立谐振子推广到含耗散的谐振子系统（热弛豫、纯退相干和Caldeira-Leggett模型），利用Moyal-Wigner相空间形式证明耗散需要导致经典界发生依赖耗散的偏移，并给出了非经典性判据保持有效的阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsirelson提出的基于动力学的量子性认证测试假设系统是孤立的，但在实际实验实现中这一假设往往不成立，因为系统不可避免地与周围环境耦合而存在耗散。因此需要将原有协议扩展到更现实的含耗散条件。

Method: 采用Moyal-Wigner相空间量子力学形式体系，将Tsirelson的动力学测试协议推广到三种标准耗散模型：热弛豫、纯退相干以及Caldeira-Leggett模型。通过相空间分析推导耗散条件下的经典边界，并计算非经典性判据的有效阈值。

Result: 研究表明，引入耗散后需要对经典界进行依赖于耗散参数的偏移修正，并给出了在该修正下非经典性见证者仍然有效的最低耗散阈值条件。

Conclusion: 该工作使基于动力学的量子性认证测试在更现实的开放量子系统场景中仍然适用，为实验上在非理想（含耗散）条件下实施这类测试提供了理论基础。

Abstract: A dynamics-based test, originally proposed by Tsirelson for the harmonic oscillator, provides a method for certifying quantumness under the assumption of a known Hamiltonian. These tests, however, are typically proposed for isolated systems, an assumption that breaks down in experimental implementation. In this paper, we extend the protocol to the harmonic oscillator coupled to standard models of dissipation: thermal relaxation, pure dephasing, and the Caldeira--Leggett model. Using the Moyal-Wigner formalism of quantum mechanics in phase space, we show that the introduction of dissipation requires a dissipation-dependent shift of the classical bound, and provide the threshold under which the nonclassicality witness retains its validity.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum Chaos and Quantum Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27350)
*Jordan Cotler,Felipe Hernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子最优输运理论的量子混沌严格框架，通过定义量子Lyapunov指数来刻画量子系统的混沌行为，并在半经典极限下恢复经典全局扩张率，为量子混沌理论建立了统一的数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典混沌通过Lyapunov指数描述邻近轨迹的指数发散，但这一框架难以直接推广到量子力学中，因为量子态的波粒二象性和量子相空间的非对易几何阻碍了经典混沌理论的直接推广。现有的量子混沌诊断工具缺乏严格的几何基础，需要一种新的数学框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 利用量子最优输运理论为量子态分布之间的距离提供了缺失的几何基础，在此基础上定义量子Lyapunov指数，使其自然避免朴素推广经典指数时遇到的发散问题；并证明在半经典极限下该指数恢复经典的全局扩张率，进而在二者一致时恢复通常的最大Lyapunov指数。

Result: 建立了经典轨迹发散与半经典相空间演化之间的紧密联系，阐明了out-of-time-order correlators（OTOC）作为量子混沌诊断的作用，证明了量子最优输运理论可作为量子混沌理论的统一数学基础，适用于从少体模型到复杂多体系统的全范围量子动力学。

Conclusion: 量子最优输运理论为量子混沌理论提供了统一的数学基础，新的量子Lyapunov指数在连接经典与量子混沌框架方面具有自洽性和有效性，为表征从简单到复杂量子系统的动力学行为提供了新工具。

Abstract: Chaos in classical systems can be characterized by Lyapunov exponents that measure the exponential divergence of nearby trajectories, but directly extending this framework to quantum mechanics has been a persistent challenge. The wavelike nature of quantum states and the non-commutative geometry of quantum phase space obstruct a straightforward generalization of classical chaos theory. Here we develop a rigorous approach to quantum chaos by leveraging quantum optimal transport theory, which provides the missing geometric foundation for measuring distances between extended quantum distributions. We define quantum Lyapunov exponents that naturally avoid divergences encountered when naïvely generalizing classical exponents, and show that in the semiclassical limit they recover the classical global expansion rate, and hence the usual maximal Lyapunov exponent when they coincide. Our framework provides a tight connection between the divergence of classical trajectories and semiclassical phase space evolution, and additionally clarifies the role of out-of-time-order correlators as diagnostics of quantum chaos. These results establish quantum optimal transport as a unifying mathematical foundation for quantum chaos theory, providing new tools to characterize dynamical behavior across the full range of quantum dynamics from simple few-body models to complex many-body systems.

</details>


### [101] [Randomness can be certified in energy-constrained semi-device-independent scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27357)
*Shashank Kumar Ranu,Lewis Wooltorton,Alastair A. Abbott,Omar Fawzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在能量约束的半设备无关（SDI）量子密码框架中，通过改编Navascués-Pironio-Acín（NPA）层级到能量约束场景，构造了猜测概率的半定规划（SDP）松弛，证明了即使准备和测量设备共享量子纠缠（即面对完全量子敌手），仍可认证正的可提取随机性下界，从而肯定地回答了该框架中随机性能否被认证的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量约束的prepare-and-measure框架是介于完全设备依赖和设备无关量子密码之间的实用折中方案。此前安全分析假设设备间最多共享经典关联，但近期工作表明敌手可在设备间预分发纠缠，发动严格更强的攻击，将可提取随机性降至此前认证的速率以下。这留下了一个基本开放问题：在此场景下随机性是否还能被认证。

Method: 作者将NPA层级适配到允许设备间共享纠缠的能量约束场景中，构造了猜测概率（guessing probability）的半定规划松弛。该松弛不限制准备设备和测量设备之间共享量子态的维度，从而能够给出可提取随机性的认证下界。

Result: 作者证明在一定的能量值范围内，这些认证下界严格为正数，表明在该框架中即使在完全量子敌手的存在下，认证随机性生成在理论上是可能的。

Conclusion: 该工作肯定地回答了能量约束SDI框架中随机性可认证性的开放问题：在允许准备和测量设备共享纠缠的情况下，仍可认证正的可提取随机性，扩展了该框架的安全分析并为其实际应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The prepare-and-measure framework based on energy constraints offers a practical middle ground between fully device-dependent and device-independent quantum cryptography. The only assumption on an otherwise uncharacterized prepare-and-measure device is that the energy of the prepared states is bounded. Existing security analyses of this framework assume that the preparation and measurement devices share at most classical correlations, and under this assumption certified lower bounds on the extractable randomness have been established. Recent work has shown that an adversary who pre-distributes entanglement between the devices can mount attacks that are strictly more powerful than those available when the devices share only classical correlations, reducing the extractable randomness below the previously certified rates. This leaves open the fundamental question of whether randomness can be certified at all in this scenario.
  We address this open question by constructing semidefinite programming relaxations of the guessing probability by adapting the Navascués-Pironio-Acín hierarchy to the energy-constrained setting where shared entanglement between the devices is permitted. Our relaxations yield certified lower bounds on the extractable randomness without enforcing any restrictions on the dimensions of the quantum state shared between the preparation and measurement devices. We show that these certified lower bounds are strictly positive for a range of energy values, thereby answering the open question affirmatively: certified randomness generation is theoretically possible in the energy-constrained SDI framework even in the presence of a fully quantum adversary.

</details>


### [102] [On supporting affine functionals for Entanglement of Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27363)
*A. S. Holevo,M. E. Shirokov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了纠缠形成度（EoF）在退化态上不一定存在全局支撑仿射泛函，即使对于最简单的两个量子比特系统（qubit-qubit）也是如此。作者利用Wootters公式和Claude Fable 5找到了一个反例，并建立了EoF在给定态存在支撑仿射泛函的充要条件，进而推导出有限秩态上EoF的Lipschitz下半连续界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前多篇文献假设EoF的凸屋顶结构以及子系统有限维性足以保证在任何态处存在全局支撑仿射泛函。这一假设被广泛用于证明EoF的性质和推导不等式，但其正确性从未被严格检验。作者旨在验证这一潜在前提的合理性，并填补关于EoF支撑泛函存在性条件的理论空白。

Method: 作者首先证明EoF在态ρ处存在支撑仿射泛函等价于EoF在该态处的Lipschitz下半连续性。利用Wootters公式精确计算二量子比特系统的EoF，借助Claude Fable 5构造一个退化态ρ，验证其不满足Lipschitz下半连续性，从而得出反例。随后分别给出有限维和无限维双体量子系统中局部及全局支撑仿射泛函存在的条件，并据此推导有限秩态上EoF的Lipschitz下半连续性界。

Result: 构造了一个二量子比特系统中的退化态ρ，证明在该态处EoF不存在全局支撑仿射泛函，推翻了此前文献中的假设。同时得到了有限维和无限维情形下局部/全局支撑仿射泛函存在的充要条件，并给出了有限秩态上EoF在有无支撑限制两种情形下的Lipschitz下半连续界。

Conclusion: 此前文献中关于"凸屋顶结构+有限维性保证全局支撑泛函存在"的假设在退化态上不成立，需要在该情形下重新审视依赖此假设得出的结论。本文给出的条件刻画了支撑泛函存在的精确边界，为EoF的Lipschitz下半连续性和相关不等式的推导提供了更严格的数学基础。

Abstract: In several articles, the authors assume that the convex roof structure of the EoF and finite-dimensionality of subsystems $A$ and $B$ guarantee the existence of the (global) supporting affine functional for the EoF at any state of the system $AB$. This means that for any state $ρ$ of $AB$ there is a Hermitian operator $Λ_ρ$ on $\mathcal{H}_{AB}=\mathcal{H}_A\otimes\mathcal{H}_B$ such that $E_F(ρ)=\mathrm{Tr}Λ_ρρ$ and $E_F(σ)\geq\mathrm{Tr}Λ_ρσ$ for any state $σ$ of $AB$. We present an explicit example showing that, when $ρ$ is degenerate, this is not true even in the simplest case when $A$ and $B$ are qubit systems. The construction is based on the fact that the existence of a supporting affine functional for the EoF at a state $ρ$ is equivalent to the Lipschitz lower semicontinuity of the EoF at this state $ρ$. We use Wootters' formula and the help of Claude Fable 5 to find a state $ρ$ of the system $AB$ for which the latter property does not hold.
  We also describe conditions for the existence the local and global supporting affine functionals for the EoF at a given state of both finite and infinite-dimensional bipartite quantum systems. These conditions allow us to find Lipschitz lower semicontinuity bounds for the EoF at a given finite rank state $ρ$ (i.e. inequalities of the form $\,E_F(ρ)-E_F(σ)\leq C_ρ\|ρ-σ\|_1$) with and without restrictions on the support of the state $σ$.

</details>


### [103] [Factorized Boolean representations for efficient quantum synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27430)
*Mehul Shah,Robert Fiszer,Marek Perkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出已最小化的布尔表达式仍保留着代数结构（项的包含关系与互补极性关系），提取这些结构可生成操作数更多但执行成本更低的量子可逆电路，关键在于最宽操作的控制数（超线性成本）而非操作总数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法假定最小化后的布尔表达式最适合映射为可逆电路，但这一假设忽略了代数结构对电路执行成本（尤其控制数）的影响，导致资源估计不准确。

Method: 提出在编译前对布尔表达式进行代数结构提取（共享因子提取），将少量宽操作转化为大量窄操作，从表示层面保证成本度量不增加，再翻译为可执行电路。

Result: 在量子搜索和因式分解算法的基准与 oracle 上，该变换在表示层面从不增加两种成本度量；翻译为电路后虽需辅助线反转带来部分回退，但仍显著优于领先的电路级优化器，且速度更快。

Conclusion: 计算的表示本身是一种可优化资源，独立于逻辑最小化和电路级优化，应在编译前进行表征层面的优化。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms promise advantages beyond classical reach, but running them on error-corrected hardware requires translating Boolean specifications into reversible circuits, and the resources that translation demands determine what is executable. Established methods minimize a Boolean expression and map it to a circuit, assuming the minimized form is best. Here we show that minimized expressions retain algebraic structure minimization cannot reach, arising from containment and complementary-polarity relationships among their terms, and that extracting it yields circuits cheaper to execute despite having more operations. The decisive quantity is not a circuit's operation count but the control count of its widest operation, a superlinear cost; extracting shared factors trades a few wide operations for many narrow ones and reduces qubit count. Across benchmarks and oracles from quantum search and factoring algorithms, at the representation level the transformation never increases either cost measure, a guarantee from its construction. Translation to an executable circuit returns part of that advantage, since auxiliary lines must be uncomputed, yet the factorized circuit still left a leading circuit-level optimizer reaching lower final counts, and faster, than unaided. The representation of a computation is therefore itself a resource, optimizable before compilation and distinct from both logic minimization and circuit-level optimization.

</details>
